# i can.
## a toddler climbing onto the couch
You can climb up all by yourself!
Hold this cushion.
Hold these cushions.
The cushion is so soft.
The cushions are all fluffy.
You pulled yourself right up!
You got up there all by yourself!
How did you get up there?
You don't need any help at all!
There are two cushions up here.
You can climb and I can too.
## a child trying to pour water from a small pitcher

Use this little pitcher.
Look at these drops!
The water is coming out.
The drops are falling down.
You did it yourself!
You poured the water.
The water poured right out.
Where is the water going?
There isn't any left in the pitcher.
All the water is in the cup now.
